7
Conclusion

This paper presents ATLAS measurements of
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
meson production in the dimuon decay channel in 5.02
TeV

\mathit{{p}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
\mathit{{p}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
and
Pb
+
Pb
collisions, for the
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
mesons with
9<p_{\text{T}}<60

GeV
and
|y|<2
. The measurements are performed for three classes of
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
mesons classified according to the presence of a
R=0.2
jet as non-isolated, isolated, and inclusive. Each of these classes is further divided into prompt and non-prompt
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
. The
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
non-isolation is defined by the presence of a jet with
p_{\text{T}}>20

GeV
within
\Delta R<0.4
from
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
. The jet
p_{\text{T}}
is corrected to the MC truth level and does not include the
p_{\text{T}}
of
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
. The presence of jets from multi-parton interactions, or from the underlying event, which may correlate the
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
with a jet not originating from the same hard process, is corrected for using the orthogonal-cone method. In this set-up, more than 60% of non-prompt
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
are found to be isolated from jets at the highest measured
p_{\text{T}}
of
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
. The isolated fraction increases systematically with decreasing
p_{\text{T}}
of
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
and reaches unity for
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
with
p_{\text{T}}=9

GeV
. For
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}

p_{\text{T}}\geq 20

GeV
, the isolated fraction is found to be approximately
10-20
% larger for prompt compared to non-prompt
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
with no differences found between
\mathit{{p}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
\mathit{{p}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
and
Pb
+
Pb
collisions. The
R_{{}\mathrm{AA}}
of
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
is measured to be consistent between the isolated and the inclusive case. The observed difference in
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}

R_{{}\mathrm{AA}}
between the non-isolated
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
and isolated
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
class has a significance of
3.5\sigma
.

These new results complement previous measurements of quarkonia–jet correlations, where events required the presence of a jet and the
p_{\text{T}}
of jets included the
p_{\text{T}}
of the
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
meson. Results presented here provide new input to constrain theoretical models describing
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
production in
\mathit{{p}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
\mathit{{p}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
and
Pb
+
Pb
collisions, and provide new information for modelling
\mathit{{J\mspace{-2mu}/\mspace{-2mu}\psi}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}
suppression in
Pb
+
Pb
collisions.
